- **Class:** Mage | **Element:** Dark
- **Creature Type:** Divine | **Role:** DPS

- **Growth Priorities:** SPD, SPI, SPD, SPI, HP, SPI...

### ⚔️ Unique Ultimate Skill:
- **Domination Gaze** (`M53301`)
  - **Action Type:** Special Attack | **Skill Type:** Attack | **Target:** Spirit low (Hits: 3, Max: 1)
  - **Description:** Gazes upon those who dare to look. Targets the enemy with lowest SPI first. Deals 3 hits of 4 DMG, applying 5 stacks of Fear and draining 5 Mana per hit. If the target's Weakness is exposed, doubles Mana gained.
  - **Synergy Tags:** `Normal Astral Evil Deity, Damage (DPS), Fear, , Runes`

### 🧬 Passive Traits, Racial Abilities & Subclasses:
1. **Normal Attack (Basic Attack):** Targets the front enemy first. Deals 1 DMG. *(ID: `BD30001`)*
2. **Heroine Feature (Mind Break):** [DMG+] Deal +1 total DMG per 15 SPI. *(ID: `BD40326`)*
3. **Racial Trait (Divine):** Target loses 3 Toughness after taking DMG. *(ID: `BD20015`)*
4. **Personality Trait (Cheerful):** [Stat] Gain +5 ATK after any Weakness is exposed. *(ID: `BD10502`)*
5. **Subclass (Dominator):** [Stat] +15 SPI. *(ID: `BD51032`)*

### 🌟 Exclusive Heroine Talents:
- **Spirit Drain** (Tier: S, ID: `TF01002`): Drain 3 SPI from enemies after casting skill. Effect doubles if Weakness is exposed.

### 🔮 Signature Relic:
- **Astral Law** (Tier: S, Max Lv: 3, ID: `XD14001_001`): Apply 1 more stack of Fear.

### 💎 Ascension Materials: Mana Crystal, Darkback, Mithril Ore, Moonlight Essence
